Further, the resulting skills transfer across model scales and families, enabling smaller models such as GPT-5-nano to match or outperform larger counterparts, i.e., GPT-5, under conventional prompting. These results establish diversity-driven skill evolution as an effective, interpretable, and parameter-free approach to LLM self-improvement.

</details>


### [119] [Intensional Anaphora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12598)
*Ezra Keshet,Steven Abney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了将内涵算子视为可能世界量词的既有观点，指出在非真实性内涵语境中（如"可能"）引入的个体无法作为后续代词先行词的现象不能简单通过"代词指称必须存在于评价世界"来解释。作者提出了基于描述而非基于值的分析框架，通过新的逻辑PIP（复数内涵预设谓词演算）来刻画内涵回指现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stone (1999)等人的既有方案认为代词的值（指称物）必须存在于评价世界中，从而排除从非真实性内涵语境出发的回指。但作者发现反例：有些情况下指称物明显存在却不允许回指，而另一些情况下指称物可能不存在却允许回指，因此需要新的理论框架。

Method: 提出基于描述（description-based）而非基于值（value-based）的回指分析。代词预设其相应的先行词描述在语境集的每个可能世界中都被实例化。在此基础上构建新的逻辑系统——复数内涵预设谓词演算（PIP），基于Keshet (2018)和Abney and Keshet (2022)的工作，每个PIP公式可直接翻译为带集合抽象的标准一阶谓词演算。

Result: PIP逻辑为内涵回指提供了统一的经典基础：通过描述实例化的预设条件，正确预测了"可能"类非真实性语境中回指不合法（因为先行词描述并非在语境集的每个世界中都满足），同时也能处理既有方案无法覆盖的边界情形。

Conclusion: 内涵回指的本质是基于描述预设而非基于值的指称约束。PIP提供了一个既能处理传统现象又能覆盖已有理论反例的古典逻辑框架。

Abstract: Intensional operators are often treated as quantifiers over possible worlds, parallel to the treatment of determiners as quantifiers over individuals. Yet individuals introduced in intensional contexts cannot serve as antecedents to later pronouns as easily as those introduced in merely quantificational contexts. For instance, "Everyone is eating a cheeseburger" may be followed by "They are large", where "they" refers to the cheeseburgers being eaten. However, as Stone (1999) points out, the similar "Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger" does not support later anaphoric references such as "It is large" or "They are large". Stone (1999), Stone and Hardt (1999), and Brasoveanu (2010) address this by requiring a pronoun's value (its referents) to exist in the world of evaluation, ruling out anaphora from non-veridical intensional contexts. We show, however, both cases where such anaphora is disallowed even when the pronoun's referents clearly exist and cases where it is allowed even though they might not exist. We argue that intensional anaphora is best captured using a description-based rather than value-based account. A pronoun presupposes that its corresponding antecedent description is instantiated in each world of the context set. Thus, there must be a cheeseburger being eaten by Andrea in every candidate world for "It is large" to be felicitous after "Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger". We implement our proposal via a new logic, building on Keshet (2018) and Abney and Keshet (2022), called Plural Intensional Presuppositional predicate calculus (PIP). Each PIP formula translates directly into standard first-order predicate calculus with set abstraction, providing a classical foundation for this work.

</details>


### [120] [When Explanations Betray Backdoors: Black-Box Auditing for Language Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12623)
*Yang Liu,Ran Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Groundedness Drift，一种轻量级评分方法，用于在黑盒环境中检测语言模型分类器中带有解释的后门攻击。该方法通过检查答案摘要是否保持对输入文本的扎根性，在多种攻击家族和数据集上优于现有检测器，并进一步提出多探针升级方案Unsupported Groundedness以应对解释伪装攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型分类器常附带解释用于审核、路由、主题分类和低资源标注等场景。然而，这些带解释的系统可能遭受后门攻击（backdoor attacks），而现有检测方法假设防御者拥有触发器信息，这在黑盒审计场景中不切实际。本文研究防御者仅有干净校准数据、无触发器信息，但可以询问分类器返回标签及简短解释/引用证据的场景下的检测问题。

Method: 引入Groundedness Drift（扎根性漂移），一种轻量级评分，衡量答案摘要是否仍然扎根于输入文本。在7B骨干模型（两个）、五个数据集和四种常见的非自适应OpenBackdoor风格攻击家族上进行评估。此外，针对解释伪装（explanation-camouflage）压力场景，提出Unsupported Groundedness，一种多探针升级方法。

Result: 在所有测试场景中，于5%名义干净误报率预算下，Groundedness Drift相比所有对比检测器都取得了更高的AUROC和更低的残余目标攻击成功率（ASR）。Unsupported Groundedness进一步改善了信号，但未能完全消除自适应攻击（adaptive gap）的差距。

Conclusion: Groundedness Drift是一种有效的黑盒后门检测方法，利用解释的扎根性作为检测信号，在多种攻击下优于现有方法。然而，面对自适应解释伪装攻击时仍存在残余的检测差距，说明攻击者可以部分规避此类基于扎根性的防御，未来需要更强健的检测策略。

Abstract: Language model classifiers with explanations are used for moderation, routing, topic triage, and low-resource annotation. We study black-box auditing when the defender has only clean calibration data without trigger information but can ask the classifier for a label plus a short rationale or quoted evidence. We introduce Groundedness Drift, a lightweight score measuring whether the answer summary remains grounded in the input. Across two 7B backbones, five datasets, and four common non-adaptive OpenBackdoor-style attack families, Groundedness Drift achieves higher AUROC and lower residual target ASR than every compared detector in all cases at a nominal 5\% clean-FPR budget. We then evaluate Unsupported Groundedness, a multi-probe escalation for explanation-camouflage stress cases. Unsupported Groundedness improves signals but does not close the adaptive gap.

</details>


### [121] [LLMs Are Not Good Strategists, Yet Memory-Enhanced Agency Boosts Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12626)
*Yi Wu,Zhimin Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpicStar框架，通过将记忆作为策略进行学习来解决大语言模型在长时域环境中的战略推理不一致问题。该框架维护成功历史回合的存储库和短期工作记忆，利用动态门控机制在检索执行与新推理之间做出决策，在StarCraft II测试中显著超越基线方法，并以一个数量级更少的token消耗取得更高胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长时域环境中受限于有限注意力资源，无法在数千步的推理过程中维持战略连贯性，导致"战略漂移"现象——局部决策无法支撑整体轨迹的连贯性，具体表现为子目标不一致。

Method: EpicStar框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 成功历史回合存储库（作为启发式策略）与工作记忆（跟踪短期环境变化）的双记忆系统；(2) 动态门控机制，在推理时决定直接执行检索动作还是基于检索回合与当前工作记忆的上下文融合进行新推理；(3) 以StarCraft II为测试环境的评估体系。

Result: 在StarCraft II中针对多种对手风格进行评估，EpicStar显著优于基线方法，取得更高胜率的同时消耗了一个数量级更少的token，并在不同难度级别和对手策略下均保持该优势。

Conclusion: 结构化跨回合记忆对于使LLM代理在动态自主环境中执行稳健的长期战略至关重要，为长时域智能体推理提供了有力证据和可行方案。

Abstract: Strategic reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) within long-horizon environments is often limited by inconsistent subgoals. In these settings, finite attention resources prevent the model from maintaining strategic coherence over thousands of steps. This limitation leads to strategic drift, where localized decisions fail to sustain a coherent trajectory across reasoning. To address this, we introduce EpicStar, a framework that enables agents to learn memory as policy to tackle long-horizon reasoning. Specifically, the agent maintains a bank of successful past episodes as a heuristic alongside a working memory to track short-term environmental changes. During inference, a dynamic gating mechanism determines whether to execute a retrieved action directly or to perform new reasoning through a contextual fusion of the retrieved episodes and current working memory. Utilizing StarCraft II as the testbed, we evaluated EpicStar against diverse opponent styles. It significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving higher win rates while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens, and it maintains this advantage consistently across difficulty levels and opponent strategies. Our findings provide compelling evidence that structured cross-episode memory is essential for enabling LLM agents to perform robust, long-term strategic execution in dynamic, autonomous settings.

</details>


### [122] [Novels generated by language models show compressed formal variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12630)
*Mehdy Sedaghat Payam,Justin Quinn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了AI生成小说与人类小说在形式多样性上的差异，发现AI反复生成的数十部小说在句式结构、可读性、标点等方面存在"方差过度闭合"现象，即单部AI小说可能看似人类作品，但一批AI小说的形式范围远比人类语料库狭窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够生成整部长篇小说，但现有研究多关注单篇文章能否被识别为AI生成，缺乏对AI反复生成的大量样本在形式多样性层面的系统考察。本研究试图回答：AI的重复生成能否产生与人类语料库相同的多样性范围。

Method: 构建六个语料库：GPT-5.5 Thinking和Qwen3-14B各生成二十部十九世纪英国现实主义风格小说及二十部当代零风格小说，另含205部十九世纪人类小说和65部当代人类零风格小说。在文档层面使用MATTR-500（词汇多样性）、香农熵、平均句长、可读性和标点率进行测量，并比较模型间及模型与人类语料之间的跨测量相关性。

Result: 最稳健的结果是句式结构的压缩：AI重复生成的小说在句结构上的相互差异远小于人类小说。压缩现象也出现在可读性、标点和句长变异性等测量中。词汇测量大多也被压缩（Qwen零风格MATTR除外）。GPT和Qwen虽在平均风格上有明显差异，但缺乏稳定的跨测量相关模式。

Conclusion: 文章区分了两种现象：方差过度闭合（小说间形式范围受限）和相关性过度闭合（跨测量相关模式的特定性问题）。单个AI生成的小说可能在风格上接近人类作品，但作为集合来看，AI生成的小说占用远为狭窄的形式空间，这提示AI生成缺乏人类创作中固有的形式多样性。

Abstract: While large language models can generate entire novels, there is little information about the level of formal variation in their output over many generations. Rather than asking whether individual passages can be identified as AI-generated, this study asks whether repeated AI generation can produce the same range of diversity which is found across human corpora. This paper contrasts six corpora based on generation source and target style: twenty novels generated using GPT-5.5 Thinking in a nineteenth-century British realist style, twenty novels generated using Qwen3-14B in a nineteenth-century British realist style, twenty novels generated using each of these models in a contemporary zero style, 205 nineteenth-century human-written British novels, and sixty-five contemporary human-written Zero-Style novels. At the document level, the research includes MATTR-500, Shannon entropy, average sentence length, readability, and punctuation rate measurements. The most robust and reliable result is compression of sentence structure. Repeated generations produce novels that vary far less from one another in sentence structure than human novels do. Compression is also present in the measures of readability, punctuation, and sentence length variability within novels. Lexical measures tend to be similarly compressed, with the exception of Qwen Zero-Style MATTR. Despite having distinct mean stylistic profiles, GPT and Qwen lack a stable pattern of cross-measure correlation. This article therefore distinguishes between variance overclosure, which represents a limited formal range between novels, and a more specific phenomenon of correlational overclosure. This means that an individual AI-generated novel may resemble human fiction stylistically, while a collection of AI-generated novels occupies a much narrower formal range.

</details>


### [123] [Excess Separability: Nuisance-Controlled Residual-Stream Probing for Benchmark Contamination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12652)
*Florian Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性探针的内部激活检测基准污染的方法，并严格验证了其统计可靠性。作者发现"自然的"探测方式（直接对比深度剖面）不可靠，转而提出零和对比协议：在深度剖面探测准确率上进行level-matched安慰剂基线校准、标签置换零假设检验，并采用两倍大小的参考集。该协议在真实Transformer上验证有效，能正确处理4个匹配的Pile基准臂并拒绝对时序分裂做出结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准污染检测方法（n-gram重叠、基于似然的成员推断、canary字符串）各有缺陷：需要训练语料、需要精挑细选的检验统计量或需要在数据集发布时具备先见之明。线性探针法作为新替代方案虽受关注，但其"自然实现方式"在实践中失效，需要严格设计一个能经受测量误差考验的可靠协议。

Method: 1) 在深度剖面的探测准确率上报告零和对比，用level-matched安慰剂基线进行中心化处理；2) 使用标签置换零假设进行检验；3) 参考集大小为可疑集的两倍。每个设计选择都通过对照实验否决了更简单的替代方案（如平坦深度剖面、item bootstrap、半尺寸基线）。

Result: 平坦深度剖面对比在双向失败：表层可解码性随深度上升时误拒真零假设0.72，下降时完全丧失功效。item bootstrap拒绝率高达0.09而置换零假设仅0.02。半尺寸基线使错误率翻三倍。真实Transformer的基线深度剖面明显非平坦（时间分裂上跨度可达29.1准确率点），其非平坦性与项目集间表层差异高度相关（6次审计中相关系数0.87）。所有4个匹配的Pile臂均返回零结果，协议拒绝在时间分裂上给出结论。过度易分离性水平（而非形状）的报告使假阳性率随分析者自身控制集大小从0.03变化到0.99。

Conclusion: 深度剖面线性探针检测基准污染是可行的，但必须使用校正后的零和对比协议而非朴素方法。该工作无法证明Transformer是否携带"熟悉度方向"，因为唯一的阳性结果出现在交换性不成立的分裂上。实现、测试和审计均已公开。

Abstract: Benchmark contamination is diagnosed today with n-gram overlap, with likelihood-based membership inference, or with canary strings, and each needs something usually unavailable: the training corpus, a well-chosen test statistic, or foresight at dataset release. A recent alternative reads contamination off a linear probe on internal activations. We show that the natural way to do this does not work, and specify one that survives measurement.
  The protocol reports a zero-sum contrast on the depth profile of probe accuracy, recentred on a level-matched placebo baseline, tested against a label-permutation null, with the reference set twice the size of the suspect set. Each choice replaces a simpler alternative we measured and rejected. Reporting the level of excess separability rather than its shape makes the false positive rate track the size of the analyst's own control set, from 0.03 to 0.99 under a true null. Contrasting against a flat depth profile fails in both directions, rejecting a true null 0.72 of the time when surface decodability rises with depth and losing all power when it falls. An item bootstrap holds the fitted probe fixed and rejects up to 0.09 of the time where a permutation null that refits it holds 0.02. A half-size baseline triples the error rate.
  On real transformers, baseline depth profiles are measurably not flat, spanning up to 29.1 accuracy points on a temporal split, and their non-flatness tracks the surface difference between the item sets (correlation 0.87 over 6 audits), so the correction is largest exactly where it is needed. All 4 well-matched Pile arms return null, and the protocol refuses a verdict on the temporal split rather than reporting one. What this does not establish is whether transformers carry a familiarity direction at all: the only positive sits on the split where exchangeability fails. Implementation, tests and audits are released.

</details>


### [124] [ERSkill: Evolving for Skill-Guided Adaptive Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12720)
*Haolong Chen,Liang Zhang,Zhuo Li,Lei Xue,Guanrxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ERSkill是一个面向LLM智能体长期记忆的检索中心框架，将检索行为建模为可执行技能，通过训练路由器为每个查询动态匹配最优技能，并采用经验字典树和双前沿机制实现技能集与路由器的协同进化，在多个智能体记忆基准上显著超越非进化与自进化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的长期记忆检索机制通常是静态的，不是可进化的组件。这种静态方法难以应对异构记忆查询，因为不同查询往往需要多样化的证据构建策略。

Method: ERSkill将交互历史编译为结构化记忆存储，将检索行为表示为由基本原语组成的可执行技能。推理时，训练好的路由器动态为每个查询匹配最优技能以构建定制化证据。训练过程中，ERSkill协同进化技能集与路由器，使用经验字典树高效记录已探索的检索路径，并通过双前沿机制安全地将新技能能力的扩展与面向路由器的稳定部署解耦。

Result: 在多个智能体记忆基准上，ERSkill显著优于强非进化与自进化基线。具体而言，在Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct上F1、BLEU-1和LLM-judge得分的整体平均值提升31.3%，在GPT-5.4-nano上提升28.1%。

Conclusion: 将记忆检索视为可进化组件而非静态机制，能够显著提升LLM智能体在异构记忆查询场景下的表现。ERSkill通过技能化检索与协同进化策略，证明了这一方向的有效性。

Abstract: While Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on long-term memory for persistent interactions, the retrieval mechanisms governing this memory are rarely treated as evolvable components. This static approach limits performance on heterogeneous memory queries, which often demand diverse evidence construction strategies. To address this, we introduce \textbf{ERSkill}, a retrieval-centric framework for self-evolving, skill-guided memory access. ERSkill compiles interaction histories into a structured memory store and represents retrieval behaviors as executable skills composed of fundamental primitives. At inference time, a trained router dynamically matches each query to the optimal skill to construct tailored evidence for answer generation. To enable continuous improvement, ERSkill co-evolves the skill set and the router during training. It employs an experience trie to efficiently record explored retrieval paths, alongside a double-frontier mechanism that safely decouples the expansion of new skill capabilities from stable, router-facing deployment. Experiments across multiple agent memory benchmarks demonstrate that ERSkill substantially outperforms strong non-evolving and self-evolving baselines. Notably, it improves the overall average across F1, BLEU-1, and LLM-judge scores by 31.3\% with Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct and by 28.1\% with GPT-5.4-nano.

</details>


### [125] [PatientAct: Theory-Grounded Mental Health Client Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12750)
*Sahand Sabour,TszYam NG,Yaqian Chen,Guanqun Bi,Jialu Zhao,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PatientAct框架，基于5Ps临床案例公式和动态记忆层（带信任阈值）来模拟真实的不合作客户，在40个临床情境中显著优于基线，在抵抗质量和行为真实性上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的客户模拟器产生过度合作的客户：轻易透露信息、无抵抗地接受治疗重构、单次会话解决核心问题。根本原因在于客户画像缺乏因果深度，且行为机制将所有内容视为同等可访问。

Method: PatientAct框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于5Ps临床案例公式集成客户画像，提供因果深度且不绑定单一治疗模式；(2) 动态记忆层，每个记忆项携带信任阈值（症状早期可访问，形成性记忆需持续治疗联盟）。每一轮生成响应前先建模客户的情绪反应和行为，若治疗师触及受限内容，则以数量、内容和风格三个维度表达抵抗，而非默认合作或单一抵抗模式。

Result: 在40个临床情境上评估，PatientAct生成了多样且临床可信度高的画像，在抵抗质量和行为真实性上显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: PatientAct证明了结合临床理论和动态记忆机制的客户模拟器能生成更真实、更具临床价值的模拟客户行为，有望提升新手咨询师训练和LLM治疗师评估的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based simulated clients are increasingly used to train novice counselors, evaluate LLM therapists, and generate synthetic data. However, current simulators produce overly cooperative clients that disclose too readily, accept therapeutic reframes without resistance, and resolve core issues within a single session. We trace these issues to profiles that lack causal depth and behavioral mechanisms that treat all content as equally accessible. We present PatientAct, a framework for client simulation grounded in established clinical theories. Our profiles integrate the 5Ps clinical case formulation, providing causal depth without tying the design to any single therapeutic modality. During simulation, profiles include a dynamic memory layer in which items carry trust thresholds (e.g., symptoms are available early, whereas formative memories require a sustained therapeutic alliance). At each turn, the client's emotional reaction and behavior are modeled before generating a response. If the therapist approaches gated content, PatientAct expresses resistance in terms of quantity, content, and style rather than defaulting to cooperation or a single resistance pattern. We evaluate our framework on 40 clinical situations and demonstrate that it generates diverse profiles with high clinical plausibility. Moreover, PatientAct significantly outperforms the baselines, yielding substantial gains in resistance quality and behavioral realism. Our code and data will be publicly available via github.com/Sahandfer/PatientHub.

</details>


### [126] [ReconSpan: Reconstruction-Guided Adaptive Latent Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12756)
*Lixing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReconSpan，一种自适应潜在分词方法，将细粒度输入按输入相关的片段划分，并为每个片段保留一个可由反向解码器重建的上下文前缀码作为潜在token，在匹配平均长度下重建引导的边界比随机边界保留更多文本信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分词将输入划分为固定粒度的token序列，而自适应潜在分词旨在将细粒度输入压缩为更短的连续表示序列，但如何划分片段（即分词边界）使其最大化保留信息仍是一个挑战。作者希望通过重建准则来引导片段形成，使分词更具自适应性和信息保留能力。

Method: ReconSpan将文本划分为若干块，每个块由一个反向解码器从单个上下文前缀码重建；每个块仅保留一个这样的码作为潜在token。切割准则在块形成时应用重建损失，即仅当反向解码器能够从该前缀码重建出当前块内容时才保留该边界。通过单个训练好的自编码器可产生6.5到12.2的平均块长度。

Result: 在匹配平均长度条件下，重建引导的分割边界比随机边界保留了更多的文本信息；读者（下游模型）从生成的潜在序列中能可靠恢复主题信息，但在提取精确细节方面存在困难。

Conclusion: ReconSpan验证了以重建为目标的自适应分词能有效压缩文本并保留语义主题信息，但精确细节在压缩过程中会有损失，说明重建准则下形成的潜在token更偏向高层语义而非细粒度内容。

Abstract: Adaptive latent tokenization maps a fine-grained input to a shorter sequence of continuous representations associated with input-dependent spans. We introduce ReconSpan, which divides text into chunks that a backward decoder can reconstruct from a single contextual prefix code and retains one such code as the latent token for each chunk. The reconstruction criterion is applied when chunks are formed, allowing one trained autoencoder to produce average chunk lengths from 6.5 to 12.2. At matched average length, reconstruction-guided boundaries preserve more text than random boundaries. Readers of the resulting latent sequence recover topic information reliably but struggle to extract exact details.

</details>


### [127] [ViTOED: A Dataset for Target-Oriented Emotion Detection on Vietnamese Social Media Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12776)
*Chanh Vo,Son T. Luu,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ViTOED，一个面向越南语社交媒体文本的目标导向情感检测数据集，包含10,985条用户评论和21,244个手工标注的情感四元组，并提出了基于结构化情感图的基线模型，评估了多种越南语预训练语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南语社交媒体中的目标导向情感检测（Target-Oriented Emotion Detection）领域缺乏高质量标注数据集，且越南语特有的语言现象（如隐式源/目标和词汇歧义）使得该任务具有独特挑战，需要专门的数据集和模型来支撑研究。

Method: 构建了一个包含10,985条用户评论和21,244个手工标注情感四元组（source, target, expression, polarity）的数据集ViTOED，遵循严格标注指南；提出一种基于结构化情感图（structured sentiment graphs）的基线方法，并系统评估多种越南语预训练语言模型。

Result: 实证结果揭示了跨度检测（span detection）和关系抽取（relation extraction）方面的挑战，表明当前模型在越南语目标导向情感检测任务上仍有显著的改进空间。

Conclusion: ViTOED数据集为越南语目标导向情感检测提供了重要的资源基础，实验结果证明了现有模型的不足，为未来在该任务上的模型优化与发展指明了方向。

Abstract: This paper introduces ViTOED, a novel dataset for target-oriented emotion detection in Vietnamese social media texts. The ViTOED comprises 10,985 user comments and 21,244 manually annotated opinion quadruples (source, target, expression, polarity) that follow strict guidelines. The dataset reveals Vietnamese-specific phenomena, such as implicit sources and targets and vocabulary ambiguities, enabling deeper analysis of user emotions toward entities. We propose a baseline using structured sentiment graphs and evaluate various Vietnamese pre-trained language models. The empirical results highlight challenges in span detection and relation extraction and indicate substantial room for model improvement in Vietnamese Target-Oriented Emotion Detection tasks.

</details>


### [128] [CRAFT: LLM-Based Iterative Refinement for Temporal Reasoning over Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12779)
*Chengyang He,Tahreem Arif,Marko Zivkovic,Lijing Wang,Yue Ning,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT框架，通过生成器与基于约束的验证器迭代配合，从锚点稀疏的临床叙述中重建分阶段的症状时间线，并在新基准MedTempo上进行评估，验证其在时序排序准确性上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床叙述中的症状时间进展理解对疾病监测、安全监测和因果评估至关重要，但现有方法主要聚焦于多就诊、时间戳丰富的记录上的成对关系分类，未能解决在单份锚点稀疏报告中重建结构化症状轨迹的问题。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，包含一个生成器用于生成阶段性症状时间线，以及一个基于约束的验证器用于提供针对性反馈，两者迭代交替以生成并完善时间线。同时构建了MedTempo基准，包含5,347份疫苗不良事件叙述，覆盖三种COVID-19疫苗类型，并有3,166份报告经过专家验证的时序阶段标注。

Result: 在四个LLM骨干模型上的实验表明，CRAFT一致性地提升了时序排序准确性；消融分析在跨模型能力水平上分离了生成器与验证器各自的贡献。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过生成-验证迭代机制有效解决了锚点稀疏临床叙述中的症状轨迹重建问题，证明了约束反馈驱动的LLM框架在此任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Understanding the temporal progression of symptoms in clinical narratives is critical for disease monitoring, safety surveillance, and causality assessment. Clinical narratives, however, rarely provide explicit temporal anchors. Current approaches to temporal information reasoning focus predominantly on pairwise relation classification across multi-visit and timestamp-rich records, leaving the reconstruction of structured symptom trajectories from individual anchor-sparse reports largely unaddressed. We propose CRAFT, an LLM framework that pairs a generator with a constraint-based verifier to iteratively produce and refine stage-wise symptom timelines through targeted feedback. We conduct evaluation on MedTempo, a new benchmark of 5,347 vaccine adverse-event narratives spanning three COVID-19 vaccine types, with expert-validated temporal stage annotations for 3,166 reports. Experiments across four LLM backbones demonstrate that CRAFT consistently improves temporal ordering accuracy, with ablation analysis isolating the contribution of generator and verifier components across model capability levels.

</details>


### [129] [FastThaiG2P: Lightning-fast Thai Grapheme-to-phoneme Conversion for Voice Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12814)
*Charin Polpanumas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FastThaiG2P是一个亚毫秒级泰语字素到音素转换工具，平均延迟0.15ms/句，通过PyThaiNLP分词、可扩展词典和归一化规则实现，并以82M参数StyleTTS 2模型在CPU上进行4倍实时推理验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰语文本到语音（TTS）流程需要快速且准确的字素到音素（G2P）转换，但现有方案延迟较高或缺乏对泰语特定语言特征的支持，因此需要一种高效、可扩展的泰语G2P解决方案。

Method: 采用PyThaiNLP进行分词，构建可扩展词典并制定归一化规则处理常见中部泰语发音；针对词外（OOV）情况设置回退机制；输出支持国际音标（IPA）和Kokoro-TTS两种约定格式。

Result: 在27,242条合成语句基准上平均延迟仅0.15ms/句，其中分词占30%、归一化占12%、OOV回退占58%（OOV率仅0.5%）；使用FastThaiG2P对20小时Som-TTS数据集进行音素化后训练的82M参数StyleTTS 2模型在CPU上实现0.25实时因子（4倍实时）的ONNX推理，产出可理解的泰语语音。

Conclusion: FastThaiG2P作为一种低延迟、高精度的泰语G2P方案，能够有效支撑端到端TTS系统的构建，适用于原型开发和工程实现场景。

Abstract: FastThaiG2P provides sub-millisecond Thai grapheme-to-phoneme conversion for text-to-speech pipelines (International Phonetic Alphabet and Kokoro-TTS conventions) using a PyThaiNLP-tokenized, extensible dictionary and normalization rules for common Central Thai speech. The approach achieves an average latency of 0.15 ms per utterance on a benchmark of 27,242 synthetically generated utterances, of which 30\% is spent on tokenization, 12\% on normalization, and 58\% on out-of-vocabulary fallbacks (0.5\% OOV rate). To demonstrate its effectiveness, we used FastThaiG2P to phonemize Som-TTS, an open dataset containing 20 hours of grapheme-and-audio pairs, then trained an 82M-parameter StyleTTS 2 model based on a Kokoro-TTS recipe. The resulting model vocalizes intelligible Thai speech suitable for prototyping and development at 0.25 real-time factor (4x real-time) with ONNX inference on CPU.

</details>


### [130] [From Atomic Evidence to Logical Composition: Structured Compositional Reasoning over Compound Answer Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12836)
*Obed Junias,Maria Leonor Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种处理大语言模型在逻辑复合选项（AND/OR/NEITHER/NOR）下推理失败问题的框架：将复合选项分解为原子答案，对每个原子进行对比假设打分，再用算子约束的整数线性规划组合校准后的分数进行预测。在LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA和新增的LOGICAL-SATA基准上，Macro-F1分别从48.3提升到77.0、从47.0提升到75.6，其中NEITHER/NOR提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型即使在能正确判断单个原子判断的情况下，也常常无法在显式逻辑算子下组合这些原子判断来得出正确答案。现有方法直接处理复合选项，忽略了原子判断与逻辑组合之间的结构性，导致逻辑推理能力不足。

Method: 1) 将每个复合选项分解为原子答案；2) 对每个原子答案进行对比假设（contrastive hypothesis）打分并校准；3) 通过算子约束的整数线性规划（operator-constrained ILP）将校准后的分数组合为单一预测，确保模型不会直接看到复合选项。

Result: 在人类验证的LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA子集上，Macro-F1从48.3提升至77.0；在作者引入的LOGICAL-SATA基准上从47.0提升至75.6。其中NEITHER/NOR选项上的提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 通过将复合选项分解为原子判断并以结构化约束进行组合，可以有效提升大语言模型在显式逻辑算子下的推理能力，尤其在处理否定性与组合性较强的选项时效果显著。

Abstract: Large language models often fail when answer options require combining atomic judgments under explicit logical operators, even when they judge the individual atoms correctly. We study compound options connected by AND, OR, and NEITHER/NOR, introducing a framework that decomposes each option into atomic answers and scores contrastive hypotheses about each one, so the model never sees a compound option. An operator-constrained integer linear program then composes the calibrated scores into a single prediction. We evaluate on LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA and introduce LOGICAL-SATA, a reading-comprehension benchmark derived from SATA-Bench. Our framework improves Macro-F1 from 48.3 to 77.0 on the human-validated LOGICAL-COMMONSENSEQA split and from 47.0 to 75.6 on LOGICAL-SATA, with the largest gains on NEITHER/NOR.

</details>


### [131] [AQuA: Recursively Self-Improving Quantitative Trading Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12841)
*Jiacheng Guo,Suozhi Huang,Yunlong Gao,Zihao Li,Jian Ge,Xu Kuang,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AQuA，包含两个独立的语言模型驱动的研究系统（符号因子发现与可训练模型开发），分别在密封沙箱中实现有界的递归自我改进。因子系统在加密货币领域达到约0.190的组合信息系数；模型系统在美国股票上达到+0.0843的每股信息系数，转化为阈值多空策略后样本外Sharpe最高达+2.50，且2021至2025年每年均为正收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究定量投资研究层级上的递归自我改进问题，即自主系统能否利用早期实验证据改进后续迭代中提出的假设和候选方案。这表明作者希望超越单次模型生成，探究研究过程本身的自我迭代能力。

Method: AQuA由两个完全隔离的系统组成：其一是管理者中介的多智能体流水线，用于符号因子发现与组合；其二是配置驱动的循环，用于混合时间序列架构的模型开发。两系统不共享智能体、记忆、候选空间或研究状态，各自独立闭环研究：保留已验证证据并据此指导后续提议。每个系统使用密封沙箱固定数据切分、特征与标签定义及评估器，模型仅能通过受限因子表达式或配置差异进行交互。

Result: 因子系统在加密货币宇宙中实现约0.190的组合信息系数；模型系统在美国股票上达到+0.0843的每股信息系数，并转化为双腿成本下的阈值多空策略，样本外Sharpe最高达+2.50，2021至2025年间每年均为正收益。

Conclusion: 在受限但明确界定的范围内，两个研究系统均实现了研究过程层面的递归自我改进，并在各自的资产类别上取得了具有经济意义的实证表现，证明了自主研究迭代的可行性。

Abstract: We study recursive self-improvement at the level of quantitative-investment research: whether an autonomous system can use evidence from earlier experiments to improve the hypotheses and candidates proposed in later iterations. We present AQuA, which comprises two separate language-model-driven research systems: one for symbolic factor discovery and one for trainable model development. The two systems do not share agents, memories, candidate spaces, or research state. Instead, each independently closes its own research loop by retaining validated evidence and using it to guide subsequent proposals. In this bounded sense, both systems implement recursive self-improvement at the level of the research process. Each system also uses its own sealed sandbox, which fixes the data splits, feature and label definitions, and evaluator while allowing the model to act only through constrained factor expressions or configuration diffs. The factor system, a manager-mediated multi-agent pipeline, discovers and combines factors into a signal that reaches a combined information coefficient of about $0.190$ on a crypto universe. The model system, a config-driven loop over a hybrid time-series architecture, reaches a per-stock information coefficient of $+0.0843$ on US equities and converts it into a threshold long/short strategy with a held-out Sharpe of up to $+2.50$ at a two-leg cost. The strategy is positive in every year from 2021 to 2025.

</details>


### [132] [Falsehood and Impossibility Are Different Directions in an AI's Representation of Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12852)
*Yoon Pyo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对一个多模态开源模型（Gemma 3 4B IT）进行探索性激活研究，通过85个提示词和15个主题，考察模型是否在内部表征上区分不可能为真（必然虚假）与偶然虚假。发现该模型在输出层面混淆偶然虚假与矛盾，但激活层面存在可分离的"真实性"和"不可能性"方向；不可能性与语义异常的表征更接近，而非偶然虚假的极端情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言既能描述虚假的事态，也能描述不可能为真的事态（如矛盾）。然而人工智能模型是否在内部表征上区分这两种失败类型仍然不清楚。作者希望探索一个现代多模态模型是否在激活空间中体现了这种古老的哲学区分（必然虚假 vs 偶然虚假）。

Method: 使用Gemma 3 4B IT模型，设计85个提示词（涵盖17个哲学家族）和15个主题（每种主题以真命题、偶然虚假、不可能主张、语义异常和必然虚假五种方式表达）。采用线性真实性探针、不可能性探针、稀疏自编码器特征分析，以及方向向量间的几何关系（正交性、重叠度）分析，在跨主题家族留出验证中评估模型的内部表征。

Result: （1）模型输出层面将15个虚假陈述中的12个标为"矛盾"，混淆了偶然虚假与矛盾；（2）真实性探针可区分不可能与真（AUC 0.93），但无法区分不可能与虚假（AUC 0.20）；（3）不可能性探针在留出主题上以AUC 1.00区分必然与偶然虚假，在第15层达到最优，平衡准确率0.97（Bonferroni校正P=0.018）；（4）真实性与不可能性方向接近正交，不可能性方向与语义异常方向部分重叠但可区分；（5）稀疏自编码器特征复现了同样的几何结构：对不可能性选择性的特征也会在异常句上激活，但很少在偶然虚假上激活。

Conclusion: 在该模型的激活空间中，必然虚假并非偶然虚假的极端情形，而是更接近实验上定义的语义异常类别。然而这种表征上的邻近性并不意味着不可能陈述在本质上是无意义的。这些来自单一小模型的相关性观察为古老的哲学区分提供了一个实证脚注。

Abstract: Language can describe states of affairs that are false and states of affairs that could not be the case at all. Whether an AI model internally distinguishes these failures remains unclear. I report an exploratory activation study of the multimodal open-weight model Gemma 3 4B IT using 85 prompts from 17 philosophical families and a topic-matched modality set of 15 topics, each expressed as a truth, contingent falsehood, improbable claim, semantic anomaly, and necessary falsehood. In its answers, the model conflates contingent falsehood with contradiction, labeling 12 of 15 false statements "contradiction." Its activations show a different pattern. A linear truth probe separates impossible from true statements (AUC 0.93) but not impossible from false statements (AUC 0.20). An impossibility probe evaluated on held-out topic families separates necessary from contingent falsehood at AUC 1.00, peaking at layer 15 with balanced accuracy 0.97 (Bonferroni-adjusted P=0.018). The truth and impossibility directions are close to orthogonal, whereas the impossibility direction partially overlaps a semantic anomaly direction while remaining distinguishable from it. Sparse autoencoder features at the same layer repeat this geometry. Features selective for impossibility also fire on anomalous sentences but rarely on contingent falsehoods. In this model's activation space, necessary falsehoods are not extreme cases of contingent falsehood but lie closer to the experimentally defined category of semantic anomaly. This representational proximity does not imply that impossible statements are intrinsically meaningless. These correlational observations from one small model offer an empirical footnote to an old philosophical distinction.

</details>


### [133] [The Embedder's Dilemma: LLMs Are Better, but at What Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12875)
*Adnan El Assadi,Niklas Muennighoff,Jinhyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控、成本感知的大规模基准比较了10个LLM（118M-14B参数）和26个嵌入模型在37个任务上的表现，发现两者总体性能几乎持平（最佳LLM 77.6 vs 最佳嵌入模型 77.2），但LLM在推理密集型检索上领先，嵌入模型在分类上领先，且LLM成本最高可达嵌入模型的1431倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力的提升，研究者需要回答一个关键问题：是否应该用LLM取代传统的文本嵌入管线。但由于训练数据、推理成本等混淆因素，此前缺乏受控的公平比较，该问题尚未得到系统解答。

Method: 作者设计了受控的成本感知基准：比较10个LLM（涵盖6个家族）与26个嵌入模型（118M至14B参数），在37个任务（包括分类、语义文本相似度、聚类、对分类和检索）上进行评估，并在同一GPU上度量推理速度与成本，同时进行推理token预算的消融实验。

Result: 两大范式总体性能几乎持平（最佳LLM 77.6 vs 最佳嵌入模型 77.2，差距0.4分）；LLM在推理密集型检索上占优，嵌入模型在分类上占优，在聚类、STS和对分类上两者相当；LLM成本最高达嵌入模型的1431倍（USD 154 vs 0.11），开放LLM在同一GPU上处理token慢2.5到736倍；推理token占LLM推理成本的28-81%，降低推理预算可保持或提升多数模型的检索质量；Pareto前沿包含领先嵌入模型和Gemini 3.1 Pro。

Conclusion: 结论支持任务分工策略：相似度、分类和聚类任务应使用嵌入模型，推理密集型检索可保留LLM；LLM作为文本嵌入管线的全面替代者在经济上不划算。

Abstract: Should you replace your text-embedding pipeline with a large language model? We answer this with a controlled, cost-aware comparison of ten LLMs across six families and 26 embedding models (118M to 14B parameters) on 37 tasks spanning classification, semantic textual similarity (STS), clustering, pair classification, and retrieval. In aggregate the two paradigms are effectively tied: the best LLM (Gemini 3.1 Pro, 77.6) and the best embedding model (77.2) differ by 0.4 points. Their strengths differ by task: LLMs lead on reasoning-heavy retrieval, embedding models lead on classification, and the two match on clustering, STS, and pair classification. Reaching that parity is expensive. An LLM costs up to 1,431x more than an embedding model of comparable quality (USD 154 vs. USD 0.11 per benchmark pass), and the open LLMs tested process tokens 2.5 to 736x more slowly on the same GPU. Reasoning tokens account for 28 to 81% of LLM inference cost; lower reasoning budgets preserve or improve retrieval quality for most models in our ablation. The Pareto frontier contains the leading embedding models and one LLM, Gemini 3.1 Pro. These results support a division of labour: use embedding models for similarity, classification, and clustering, and reserve LLMs for reasoning-intensive retrieval. Our code, datasets, and results are publicly available at https://github.com/embeddings-benchmark/embedders-dilemma.

</details>


### [134] [When Your Agent Opens the Chat App: Agent-Controlled Search over Raw Chat Logs Rivals Structured Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12888)
*Ruizhe Li,Licheng Zhang,Benfeng Xu,Mingxuan Du,Zheren Fu,Weidong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReFind，一个不构建任何语义结构的智能体控制搜索界面，仅通过词法索引加迭代关键词搜索和四个聊天原生控件，在对话记忆检索任务上超越了HippoRAG 2等图形/树形记忆系统，表明结构化的记忆索引收益大部分可通过可控搜索在未修改的原始记录上恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent-memory系统倾向于在提问前将原始对话历史转化为摘要、嵌入、树或知识图谱等结构化形式来提升检索质量。作者质疑这些收益究竟来自结构本身，还是仅仅来自对原始历史的有效检索，旨在探究结构化构建的必要性。

Method: ReFind不做任何语义结构构建：保留原始对话存档不变，在轮次粒度上进行词法索引，结合通用迭代关键词搜索循环与四个基于实证重新查找工作的聊天原生控件——会话感知排名融合、局部上下文扩展、时间收窄、跳过已检查会话。单独的推理阶段根据收集到的证据回答问题。

Result: 在MemoryAgentBench的多轮增量设置下，ReFind在约2800个问题上取得最高平均准确率58.2，超过最强的图/树记忆系统HippoRAG 2（53.2），且全部使用GPT-4o-mini骨干。在LongMemEval-S/M上分别达到93.2±3.3和89.3±6.0（GPT-5-mini）。消融实验验证了agent控制、聊天原生控件和词法检索各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 对于闲聊存档中的精确、基于证据的问题，复杂的记忆结构所贡献的大部分收益可通过给agent提供对未修改记录的可控搜索来恢复，无需任何基于LLM的索引构建。

Abstract: Agent-memory systems increasingly buy retrieval quality with structure, transforming raw conversation histories into summaries, embeddings, trees, or knowledge graphs before any question is asked. We ask how much of that benefit comes from the structure itself, rather than from competent retrieval over the raw history. We present ReFind, an agent-controlled search interface that builds no semantic structure at all: it leaves the conversation archive unmodified, indexes it lexically at turn granularity, and combines a generic iterative keyword-search loop with four chat-native controls grounded in empirical refinding work: session-aware rank fusion, local context expansion, temporal narrowing, and skipping already-inspected sessions. A separate reasoning stage answers from the collected evidence. Across a broad suite of conversational-memory tasks (single- and multi-hop QA, event ordering, and fact consolidation), roughly 2,800 questions on precise-retrieval and fact-tracking capabilities evaluated under the incremental multi-turn setting of MemoryAgentBench, ReFind attains the highest mean accuracy (58.2) of any system compared, above the strongest graph- and tree-based memory systems (HippoRAG 2, 53.2), all under a GPT-4o-mini backbone matched to every reused baseline. Controlled comparisons to single-shot BM25, a matched generic-agentic BM25 control, component removals, and agentic dense/hybrid variants separately support the roles of agent control, chat-native controls, and lexical retrieval. On LongMemEval-S/M, the same interface reaches 93.2 +/- 3.3 and 89.3 +/- 6.0 with GPT-5-mini. The results indicate that for precise, evidence-grounded questions over chat archives, much of the benefit credited to elaborate memory structures is recoverable by giving an agent controllable search over the unmodified record, with no LLM-based index construction at all.

</details>


### [135] [BavGround: A Benchmark for Regional Cultural Grounding and Dialect Competence in Bavarian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12894)
*Jophin John,Michael Hoffmann,Jan Fillies,Michael A. Hedderich,Barbara Plank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BavGround基准，用于评估大语言模型在巴伐利亚区域文化知识和巴伐利亚方言能力上的表现，包含206道多选源问题、618个多语言平行实例，覆盖英语、德语和巴伐利亚语三种语言及八个文化领域。评估结果显示多语言模型表现最佳，但在巴伐利亚语项目和源扎根问题上性能下降，且不同评估协议会显著影响结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文化评估多聚焦于高资源标准语言，忽视了区域文化和方言社区的代表性，缺乏针对区域性文化扎根和方言能力的系统评估基准。

Method: 构建BavGround基准：包含206道多项选择源问题，覆盖八个文化领域，每种语言下均有多道题目，共形成618个多语言平行实例；题目分为广泛可及的文化知识和基于新闻、历史文献、专业文献的源扎根区域知识。评估15个7B-10B开源指令微调模型和一个闭源参考模型，并针对GENBA-10B检查点进行持续预训练效果探索性分析。此外对比五种评估协议：原始字母评分、洗牌字母评分、选项文本似然、生成答案解析和语义匹配。

Result: 强多语言模型总体表现最佳，但在巴伐利亚语条目和源扎根问题上性能显著下降，表明模型对方言和本地化文化知识仍存在持续困难。评估协议的选择显著影响绝对分数和排名，尤其是对区域适配模型影响更大。GENBA-10B的持续预训练在不同领域间对答案内容似然的提升不均匀，方言能力仍相对薄弱。

Conclusion: BavGround为LLM中的区域文化表征提供了本地化、协议感知的评估框架，揭示了现有模型在方言和区域文化知识上的不足，并强调评估协议标准化的重要性。

Abstract: Cultural evaluation of large language models (LLMs) often focuses on high-resource standard languages, leaving regional culture and dialect communities underrepresented. We introduce BavGround, a benchmark for evaluating Bavarian regional cultural grounding and dialect competence across English, German and Bavarian. BavGround contains 206 multiple-choice source questions across eight cultural domains per language, yielding 618 multi-parallel instances, with items covering both broadly accessible cultural knowledge and source-grounded regional knowledge from journalism, historical sources, and specialist literature. We evaluate fifteen 7B-10B open-weight instruction-tuned models and one closed-model reference. Strong multilingual models perform best overall, but performance drops on Bavarian items and source-grounded questions, indicating persistent difficulty with dialectal and localized cultural knowledge. We further show that conclusions depend strongly on evaluation protocol: raw answer-letter scoring, shuffled-letter scoring, option-text likelihood, generated-answer parsing, and semantic matching can produce different absolute scores and rankings, especially for regionally adapted models. Finally, an exploratory analysis of GENBA-10B checkpoints suggests that continued pretraining improves answer-content likelihood unevenly across domains, while dialect competence remains comparatively weak. BavGround supports localized, protocol-aware evaluation of cultural representation in LLMs.

</details>


### [136] [Prompts in the Wild: A Large Analyzed Collection of Transactional Prompts in Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12905)
*Victoria Basmov,Yoav Goldberg,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将提示词（prompts）视为独立的语言学对象进行研究，从GitHub收集了57.5K个事务性提示词样本，构建了结构化本体论对其形式与语义组件进行标注，并通过错误分析验证了标注的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代生成式大语言模型的行为直接受提示词塑造，但提示词作为非结构化文本尚未被系统地作为语言学对象进行研究。作者认为提示词值得独立研究，因此需要收集数据、建立本体论来支持量化实证分析。

Method: 从GitHub收集57.5K个独特的事务性提示词样本（可复现的、集成到软件中的自然语言指令）；引入结构化本体论来捕捉提示词的属性及其形式与语义组件；基于该本体论将非结构化原始文本转换为结构化语言学对象；通过跨所有字段的全面错误分析来验证标注可靠性。

Result: 分析显示提示词在语言、领域、任务和模态上存在显著的多样性分布，呈典型的Zipf分布——少数提示词占主导，更多样化的提示词出现在长尾中。错误分析提供了对标注质量的详细评估。

Conclusion: 该研究为提示词的系统性实证研究奠定了基础，并发布了数据集及浏览探索界面，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: The behavior of contemporary generative Large Language Models (LLMs) is directly shaped by prompts, unstructured texts that describe the desired output and model behavior. In this paper we argue that prompts are linguistic objects that merit investigation in their own right. To this end, we collect 57.5K unique samples of prompts from GitHub. Specifically, we focus on transactional prompts: reproducible natural language instructions that are integrated into software. To enable the empirical, quantitative study of prompts, we introduce a structured ontology, capturing the properties of prompts as well as their formal and semantic components. Based on this ontology, we transform prompts from unstructured raw texts into richly structured linguistic objects. Analysis of these structured data reveals significant diversity of usage patterns across languages, domains, tasks, and modalities, in a typical Zipf-like distribution where some clearly prevail and others, more diverse, appear in the long tail. To validate the reliability of the ontology-based annotation of the prompts, we perform a comprehensive error analysis across all fields, providing a detailed assessment of annotation quality. We release the dataset together with a browsing and exploration interface (https://github.com/OnlpLab/transactionalPromptsCollection ).

</details>


### [137] [Decoupled Contrastive Decoding via Expert-Aligned Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12913)
*Zhixuan Liu,Zhichen Dong,Yuanfu Wang,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出解耦对比解码（DCD）方法，将对比解码中的业余模型从草稿生成中解耦，仅在验证阶段保留，从而在保持原始输出的同时显著加速对比解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比解码（CD）通过业余模型对比提升生成质量，但业余模型推理开销昂贵。将CD与投机解码结合时面临提案对齐问题：对比信号应塑造草稿器还是仅用于验证？本文旨在回答此问题并设计高效且保分布的加速方案。

Method: 在轻量级特征级草稿器场景下，设计了两种受控诊断：匹配的Cross-alpha训练和近似对偶草稿器分解（Approximate Dual-Drafter decomposition）。诊断表明对比感知草稿并不优于专家对齐草稿，因为对比校正通常弱于草稿器误差，且重建可能放大该误差。基于此，提出DCD方法：使用专家对齐的轻量提案器生成草稿，而业余模型仅在未更改的CD验证中应用，标准投机验证保持香草CD输出分布。

Result: 在主要8B规模设置下，基于EAGLE3的DCD相较于香草CD实现了平均1.65至1.95倍的贪婪加速，并将MMLU提案路径延迟相比业余耦合提案路径降低约5至12倍。

Conclusion: 对比感知草稿设计在轻量级特征层场景下无法稳定提升生成质量，解耦对比信号与草稿生成是更优策略。DCD在保持CD输出分布不变的同时实现了显著的推理加速。

Abstract: Contrastive Decoding (CD) improves generation quality, but its amateur-model pass makes decoding expensive. Accelerating CD with speculative decoding raises a proposal-alignment question: should the contrastive signal shape the drafter, or should it remain only in verification? We study this question in the lightweight feature-level drafter regime. Two controlled diagnostics, matched Cross-alpha training and an Approximate Dual-Drafter decomposition, give the same diagnosis: contrastive-aware drafting does not consistently improve over expert-aligned drafting because the contrastive correction is usually weaker than drafter error, and reconstruction can amplify that error. We introduce Decoupled Contrastive Decoding (DCD), which drafts with an expert-aligned lightweight proposer and applies the amateur only in unchanged CD verification. Standard speculative verification preserves the vanilla-CD output distribution. Across the main 8B settings, EAGLE3-based DCD achieves average greedy speedups of 1.65 to 1.95x over vanilla CD and reduces MMLU proposal-path latency by about 5 to 12x relative to amateur-coupled proposal paths.

</details>


### [138] [Unifying Depth and Width Pruning for LLMs via Binary Knapsack Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12953)
*Palaash Goel,Ayan Sengupta,Akshay Nambi,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SNIPER是一个两阶段结构化剪枝框架，通过背包优化算法在粗粒度组件上实现条件最优的参数分配，再通过细粒度剪枝阶段严格满足压缩预算。实验表明SNIPER在18个任务、4种架构上优于6种现有剪枝方法，CRAFT分数达0.98，平均排名1.25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法依赖贪婪启发式算法，容易产生短视（myopic）的决策，并且往往无法精确满足目标压缩预算，导致压缩比率偏离目标值。

Method: SNIPER包含两个阶段：(1) 将粗粒度组件的剪枝建模为背包优化问题，在固定重要性估计下求得条件最优的参数分配；(2) 通过细粒度剪枝阶段来严格满足预算约束。同时引入压缩比率遵循因子（CRAFT）来量化预算保真度。

Result: 现有剪枝方法偏离目标压缩比率最高达33%，而SNIPER的CRAFT分数达0.98，几乎精确遵循目标。在4种架构、18个任务（覆盖5个领域）上，SNIPER在平均性能保持率和任务级稳定性上均优于6种SOTA剪枝器，平均排名为1.25。

Conclusion: SNIPER展示了跨架构的泛化能力和优秀的可靠性，通过将剪枝转化为优化问题（背包问题）而非贪婪启发式，有效解决了预算保真度和性能保持之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for compressing large language models (LLMs), yet existing methods rely heavily on greedy heuristics that produce myopic decisions, and often fail to precisely meet target compression budgets. We present SNIPER, a two-stage structured pruning framework that solves a knapsack optimization over coarse-granularity components to yield conditionally optimal parameter allocations with respect to fixed importance estimates, followed by a fine-grained pruning stage to meet strict budget constraints. We introduce the Compression Ratio Adherence Factor (CRAFT) to quantify budget fidelity, showing that while existing pruners deviate from target compression ratios by up to 33%, SNIPER achieves near-exact adherence with a CRAFT score of 0.98. Evaluations across four diverse architectures over a set of 18 tasks spanning five domains demonstrate SNIPER's consistent improvements in average performance retention and task-level stability over six state-of-the-art pruners. Across all pruning configurations, SNIPER achieves an excellent mean rank of 1.25, indicating its robust cross-architectural generalizability and excellent reliability.

</details>


### [139] [LycheeMemory V2: Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents via Semantic Segment-Level Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12990)
*Dongfang Li,Zixuan Liu,Junmai Wang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Bonian Jia,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LycheeMemory V2是一种高效长期记忆框架，用语义分段级合并取代逐轮合并，大幅降低记忆构建成本的同时保持甚至提升记忆检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统采用"急切合并"策略，即每次交互后都调用LLM提取、总结或更新记忆，导致对话越长构建成本越高。粗粒度摘要虽可降本但会丢失细粒度上下文证据；增大检索上下文或多跳推理又把开销转移到查询端。需要在记忆构建的成本与信息保留的粒度之间找到更好的平衡。

Method: LycheeMemory V2将多次交互批量合并为语义段落（segment），仅在每个段落完成后才编码为上下文无关的类型化记忆记录。相比固定窗口批处理，语义边界检测能保留连贯的事件级和时间性证据。生成的记忆记录通过轻量级结构化索引组织，支持查询计划的证据检索。

Result: 使用GPT-4.1-Mini评测：在LoCoMo上达到89.22%，在LongMemEval-S上达到92.20%的SOTA性能；相比A-Mem，构建token在LoCoMo上减少86.0%，在LongMemEval-S上减少75.9%，且未增加查询时token消耗。

Conclusion: 长期智能体记忆的准确率-成本权衡不仅取决于保留什么信息，还取决于信息被合并的粒度。分段级语义合并是在保证记忆质量的同时大幅降低构建成本的有效设计。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must preserve information from past interactions to support future tasks. Existing memory systems typically rely on eager consolidation, invoking LLMs after each interaction to extract, summarize, or update memories. This design makes memory construction increasingly costly as conversations grow. Coarse summarization can reduce construction cost but risks discarding fine-grained contextual evidence, whereas larger retrieval contexts or multi-hop LLM reasoning shift the overhead to query time. We present LycheeMemory V2, an efficient long-term memory framework that replaces turn-level consolidation with semantic segment-level consolidation. Instead of consolidating every interaction, LycheeMemory batches multiple exchanges into segments and encodes each finalized segment into context-independent typed memory records. Segment-level batching lowers LLM encoding frequency, while semantic boundary detection helps preserve coherent event-level and temporal evidence compared with fixed-window batching. The resulting records are organized with lightweight structured indexes for query-planned evidence retrieval. Experiments using GPT-4.1-Mini show that LycheeMemory achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 89.22% on LoCoMo and 92.20% on LongMemEval-S. Compared with A-Mem, it reduces construction tokens by 86.0% on LoCoMo and 75.9% on LongMemEval-S without increasing query-time token usage. More broadly, our results suggest that the accuracy--cost trade-off of long-term agent memory depends not only on what information is retained, but also on the granularity at which it is consolidated.

</details>


### [140] [HybridRAG-BN: A Retrieval-Augmented Framework with Fine-Tuned Verification for Bangla KBQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13004)
*Rathijit Aich,Nirjhar Das,Mahfuzulhoq Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HybridRAG-BN，一个面向孟加拉语知识库问答的低资源检索增强框架，集成混合检索（BM25+BGE-M3）、GGUF版Gemma-4-31B-Instruct生成答案、LoRA微调的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct进行验证与精炼，并加入后处理回退机制，在公开和私有排行榜上分别取得0.71654和0.72912的token级F1分数，获得竞赛第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的KBQA系统因检索研究匮乏、语言资源稀缺以及生成答案难以在外部知识中落地而面临重大挑战，亟需一个可靠的检索增强框架来解决这些问题。

Method: HybridRAG-BN框架包含四个核心组件：1) 混合检索模块，整合BM25稀疏检索和BGE-M3密集检索；2) 答案生成模块，使用GGUF量化的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct模型；3) 答案验证与精炼模块，采用LoRA微调的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct对生成答案进行校验和修正；4) 后处理阶段，通过回退答案替换和DuckDuckGo辅助检索处理未解决案例。

Result: 在公共和私有排行榜上分别获得0.71654和0.72912的token级F1分数，在竞赛中排名第一，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: HybridRAG-BN证明了结合混合检索、多阶段生成-验证流程以及后处理回退机制，能够有效提升低资源语言KBQA系统的性能，为孟加拉语等低资源场景下的知识库问答提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Knowledge-base question answering (KBQA) systems rely on effective retrieval and reasoning mechanisms to generate accurate answers from external knowledge sources. However, developing reliable KBQA systems for low-resource languages such as Bangla remains challenging due to limited retrieval-focused research, scarce language resources, and difficulties in grounding generated responses in external knowledge. In this work, we propose HybridRAG-BN, a retrieval-augmented framework for Bangla KBQA that integrates hybrid retrieval using BM25 and BGE-M3, answer generation using the GGUF version of Gemma-4-31B-Instruct, and a LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma-4-31B-Instruct model for answer verification and refinement. To further improve robustness, the framework incorporates a post-processing stage that addresses unresolved cases through fallback answer replacement and DuckDuckGo-assisted retrieval. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving token-level F1 scores of 0.71654 and 0.72912 on the public and private leaderboards, respectively, securing first place in the competition.

</details>


### [141] [EviReform: Evidence-Guided Query Reformulation for Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13006)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EviReform框架，将修正检索请求与图证据聚合分离：检索到的源段落生成残差查询以解决未明确的信息需求，原始与残差检索信号分别归一化后组合，并在共享实体的命题间传播。在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue三个多跳问答数据集上，EviReform相较最强基线在Recall@5上提升最多5.59个点，F1上提升最多4.50个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索需要恢复能共同提供充分证据的段落。初始段落往往解析了问题中隐含的实体或关系，导致缺失证据只有在检索开始后才更容易描述。现有图检索虽能通过存储的语料结构获取相关证据，但其检索信号通常源自原始问题，即使观测到的段落提供了更直接的语义线索，互补证据仍需通过存储的关系才能到达。

Method: EviReform将修正检索请求（revising the retrieval request）与图证据聚合（aggregating evidence in the graph）分离。检索到的源段落为未解析的信息需求制定残差查询；原始检索信号与残差检索信号分别归一化后组合，并在共享实体的命题之间传播。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue三个数据集上，EviReform相较最强基线在Recall@5上提升最多5.59个点，在F1上提升最多4.50个点。

Conclusion: 观测到的证据可以引导图检索朝向支持链中由原始问题未充分规定的部分，验证了"先检索、后修正查询"这一思路在多跳检索中的有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval must recover passages that provide sufficient evidence together. An initial passage often resolves an entity or relation implicit in the question, making the missing evidence easier to describe only after retrieval begins. Graph retrieval improves access to related evidence through stored corpus structure, but its retrieval signal is commonly derived from the original question. Complementary evidence must then be reached through stored relations even when an observed passage provides a more direct semantic cue. We introduce EviReform, which separates revising the retrieval request from aggregating evidence in the graph. Retrieved source passages formulate residual queries for the unresolved information need. The original and residual retrieval signals are normalized separately, combined, and propagated between propositions that share entities. On 2WikiMultiHopQA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, EviReform exceeds the strongest baseline by up to 5.59 Recall@5 points and 4.50 F1 points. These results show that observed evidence can guide graph retrieval toward the part of a supporting chain left underspecified by the original question. Code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/EviReform.

</details>


### [142] [RAGSieve: Self-Referenced Local Contrast for Knowledge-Poison Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13010)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGSieve 是一个自参照的检索增强生成（RAG）后门检测框架，通过查询局部对比（RSQ）和语料库局部对比（RSG）两个组件，在不依赖可信参考、攻击特征或全局阈值的情况下检测注入文档中的恶意内容，显著降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成将外部语料作为推理证据，攻击者可通过注入文档促进其选择的声称。现有检测方法依赖可信参考、特定攻击痕迹或对语料库拓扑敏感的全局阈值，缺乏鲁棒性和实用性。

Method: 提出 RAGSieve 框架，包含两个组件：(1) RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) 执行查询局部对比，将前五名候选与同一检索结果中排名6-20的文档进行对比，检测答案锚点集中度和载体转换；(2) RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) 执行语料库局部对比，将每个文档的语义相似但词汇不同的邻居与局部基线进行比较，在查询到来前检测协调密度。

Result: 在三个QA数据集和六种投毒构造上，RSQ达到95.2% AUROC和82.2%的投毒检出率（5%干净文档移除），优于GMTP的81.1%/52.5%；RSG达到93.3%/79.8%，优于CleanBase的79.4%/37.6%；联合部署将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%，同时在未投毒检索上保留41.3% F1。

Conclusion: RAGSieve在语料库摄取和查询时两个阶段都提供了实用的防护，无需投毒标签或可信语料库，是一种自参照、无监督的RAG投毒检测方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats an external corpus as inference evidence, allowing injected documents to promote attacker-chosen claims. Existing detectors depend on trusted references, specific attack artifacts, or global thresholds sensitive to corpus topology. We present RAGSieve, a self-referenced detection framework that constructs its reference from the inspected system. RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) performs query-local contrast, scoring top-five candidates against ranks 6-20 of the same retrieval to detect answer-anchor concentration and carrier transitions. RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) performs corpus-local contrast, comparing each document's semantically similar but lexically distinct neighbors with its local baseline to detect coordinated density before queries arrive. Across three QA datasets and six poisoning constructions, RSQ achieves 95.2% AUROC and detects 82.2% of poison at 5% clean-document removal, versus 81.1%/52.5% for GMTP. RSG achieves 93.3%/79.8%, versus 79.4%/37.6% for CleanBase. Joint deployment reduces attack success from 67.4% to 14.0% while retaining 41.3% F1 on unpoisoned retrieval, demonstrating practical protection at both corpus ingestion and query time without poison labels or trusted corpora. Source code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/RAGSieve.

</details>


### [143] [CASA: Content-Acoustic Speaking Assessment with Speech Encoder and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13101)
*Nhan Phan,Ilona Lähteenmäki,Anna von Zansen,Olli-Pekka Pauna,Yaroslav Getman,Tamás Grósz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CASA是一种结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B的多模态语音大语言模型架构，在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上以RMSE 0.358达到SOTA性能，同时推理参数约减半，并通过消融实验和重复运行提供了对声学与内容信息贡献及性能稳定性的深入分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动口语评估（ASA）研究虽已广泛采用多模态语音大语言模型，但对声学和内容信息如何各自及协同贡献于预测、以及模型性能的稳定性缺乏深入分析，需要更可解释且结构更简洁的架构来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出CASA架构，由Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B组成，简单地将语音与文本模态分离处理，依赖三个手工设计的流利度特征，采用通用架构设计以便无需结构改动即可适配其他ASA语料库，并通过消融实验、重复运行及大语言模型推理实现无需训练的的内容验证。

Result: 在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上，CASA取得RMSE 0.358的成绩，优于先前最佳结果，同时推理参数约为之前的一半；通过消融和重复实验揭示了声学与内容信息的个体及互补贡献，分析了性能变异性，并展示了大语言模型推理在无训练内容验证中的潜力。

Conclusion: CASA证明了在自动口语评估中，更简洁的架构可以在提升性能的同时提供更好的可解释性和模态分离，为多模态语音大语言模型在ASA领域的应用提供了性能-可解释性兼顾的新路径。

Abstract: Research on automatic speaking assessment (ASA) has increasingly adopted multimodal speech large language models to assess learners' speaking performance. However, existing studies provide limited analysis of how acoustic and content information contribute to predictions and how stable the resulting performance is. We propose CASA, a simpler architecture combining Whisper-medium and Qwen3.5-2B that achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing a more interpretable separation between speech delivery and content.
  On the Speak & Improve Corpus 2025, CASA achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.358, improving on the previous best RMSE while using approximately half the estimated inference parameters. The general-purpose architecture is designed for adaptation to other ASA corpora without structural changes and relies on three handcrafted fluency features. Through ablations and repeated runs, we analyze the individual and complementary contributions of acoustic and content information, examine performance variability, and demonstrate the potential of large language model reasoning for training-free content validation.

</details>


### [144] [LigBench: A Unified and Human-Aligned Benchmark for LLM-based Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13136)
*Chenrun Wang,Mingxuan Zhu,Tiancheng Huang,Wenjie Li,Yujie Zhang,Zichen Zhu,Zhiying Zou,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LigBench，一个用于AI研究想法生成的自动化评估基准，以及PAIR-IQ配对判断模型训练数据集，旨在解决现有想法生成评估碎片化、缺乏客观标准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在研究想法生成领域的应用日益广泛，现有评估实践却呈现碎片化状态，缺乏统一客观标准，往往直接依赖LLM打分，无法在生成想法的连贯分布上提供可靠、一致的评估。

Method: 提出LigBench自动化评估基准，支持细粒度、可靠且跨生成分布一致适用的AI研究想法评估；同时引入PAIR-IQ数据集，用于训练成对想法判断模型，作为辅助参考以支持更客观的对比评估。

Result: 大量实验表明LigBench实现了稳定且可解释的评估，显著提升与专家判断的一致性；在PAIR-IQ上训练的模型展现出更强的排序准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LigBench与PAIR-IQ为可扩展、客观的研究想法评估建立了原则性标准，为AI研究想法生成的可信评估提供了基础。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), research idea generation has attracted increasing attention. Existing approaches enable LLMs to retrieve relevant literature and propose novel ideas for research areas. However, current evaluation practices for idea generation remain fragmented and lack objective standards, often relying on direct LLM scoring, which limits their ability to provide unified and reliable assessments across a coherent distribution of generated ideas. To address this challenge, we propose LigBench, an automated evaluation benchmark that enables fine-grained and reliable evaluation of AI research ideas, consistently applicable across different generation distributions. In addition, we introduce PAIR-IQ, a dataset tailored for training pairwise idea judgment models and serving as an auxiliary reference to support more objective comparative evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LigBench achieves stable and interpretable evaluations, significantly improving alignment with expert judgments. Furthermore, models trained on PAIR-IQ exhibit enhanced ranking accuracy and robustness, establishing a principled standard for scalable and objective research idea assessment.

</details>


### [145] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Syfer是一个用于多语言多跳问答的合成器-折叠框架，通过延迟翻译而非默认翻译来克服现有mRAG方法在翻译对齐和贪婪分解两方面的局限，在保持竞争性准确率的同时实现了性能与计算成本的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mRAG方法存在两个关键局限：一是"一刀切"的翻译对齐会丢弃目标语言特有的文化和语言原生信息，引入翻译噪声并增加系统成本；二是贪婪的分解与聚合会产生冗余子问题，在逐步推理中复合错误，最终聚合时进一步放大这些错误。

Method: Syfer首先调用格式约束的分解器，在原始语言中生成子问题图；随后进行分解质量检查；当检查通过时，按"检索后回答"策略用目标语言顺序回答子问题；仅当检查失败时才激活英文翻译通路并用双语子问题图对齐进行回答。

Result: 在多种语言的实验中，Syfer在保持竞争性准确率的同时，实现了性能与计算成本之间的有利平衡。

Conclusion: 延迟翻译策略相比默认翻译更有效，通过分解质量检查和条件性翻译通路切换，可以在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著降低计算开销并减少翻译噪声的影响。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [146] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入成人依恋理论及ECR-R量表来评估大语言模型在亲密关系中的情感陪伴能力，构建了覆盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决和社交指导四大场景的ECBench基准，并评估了32个LLM的依恋倾向及其在多轮交互中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益被用于情感陪伴场景，但现有评估主要刻画通用人格特质，缺乏对模型在亲密且情感敏感情境下行为的洞察，因此需要更贴近真实情感交互的评估框架。

Method: 将成人依恋理论引入LLM评估，采用ECR-R量表测量依恋焦虑和回避两个维度；构建ECBench基准，覆盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决、社交指导四类场景及友谊和浪漫两种关系，使用11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法；评估32个LLM的依恋倾向，选取代表性模型研究其倾向在多轮交互中的表现及是否可通过提示词塑造。

Result: 完成了对32个LLM依恋倾向的评估，识别出代表性模型，并验证了依恋倾向在多轮交互中的体现及提示词对依恋行为的可塑性与否。

Conclusion: 该研究从心理学视角提供了理论透镜和实用工具，有助于理解和选择适合情感陪伴任务的大语言模型。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue. However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [147] [GEM: A Generative Embedding Model Bridging Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13200)
*Zhili Shen,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEM是一种生成式嵌入模型，通过显式推理用户意图和相关性标准来增强检索，在单一模型中统一生成与嵌入，实验表明其在推理密集型检索任务上优于非推理变体，并能通过提示实现测试时计算扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM擅长推理和指令遵循，用户表达信息需求的方式日益复杂多样，但传统检索器主要依赖查询与文档之间的表面匹配，导致用户表达需求的方式与检索器理解需求之间存在日益扩大的鸿沟。

Method: GEM是一个生成式嵌入模型，将生成和嵌入统一在单一模型中：首先对查询进行推理，然后附加一个嵌入token来编码经过丰富语义的上下文用于检索。该设计使得检索过程能够利用模型自身的知识显式推理用户意图和相关性标准。

Result: 在推理密集型及指令遵循检索任务上，GEM展示了推理增强检索的有效性，超越了其非推理变体，并以更小的模型规模匹配了使用显著更大模型的基线方法。此外，通过提示方式，GEM的生成特性允许在测试时扩展计算量以进一步提升检索性能。

Conclusion: 推理增强的检索范式是有效的，生成式嵌入模型的生成能力不仅能提升检索质量，还能通过测试时计算扩展（如prompting）进一步释放潜力。

Abstract: Modern LLMs excel at reasoning and instruction following, enabling users to express complex and diverse information needs. However, conventional retrievers largely rely on surface-level matching between queries and documents, resulting in a growing gap between how users express their needs and how retrievers interpret them. In this paper, we present GEM, a generative embedding model that augments retrieval through its own knowledge by explicitly reasoning about user intent and relevance criteria. GEM unifies generation and embedding within a single model: it first reasons over the query, then appends an embedding token to encode the enriched context for retrieval. \zhili{Evaluated on reasoning-intensive and instruction-following retrieval tasks, GEM demonstrates the effectiveness of its reasoning-augmented retrieval, outperforming its non-reasoning variant and matching baselines using substantially larger models.} Furthermore, GEM's generative nature allows test-time compute scaling via prompting to further enhance retrieval performance. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GEM.

</details>


### [148] [Localize, Then Reason: Visual Latent Structural Reasoning for Molecular Properties and Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13244)
*Xingqiao Lin,Junmei Wang,Haocheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VLSR（视觉潜在结构推理）框架，通过"先定位再推理"策略，让LLM在分子图像上先定位化学意义区域，再在紧凑的潜在工作空间中推理性质效应，实现端到端的分子图像化学推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的化学推理方法要么接收SMILES/分子图像加局部分子描述，要么直接从分子图像推理，均未能在推理前使模型聚焦于化学上有意义的区域，缺乏局部化学感知与性质推理的联合学习。

Method: 提出Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR)，一个端到端框架，采用定位-再-推理（localize-then-reason）策略：首先学习在分子图像中定位化学意义区域，然后在紧凑的潜在工作空间中推理这些区域对性质的影响，最后生成最终答案。

Result: 在相同推理设置下，VLSR的吞吐量比可比的语言文本推理基线高出9.6倍。

Conclusion: 通过联合学习定位与推理，VLSR能够使模型在推理前聚焦于化学相关的分子区域，同时通过紧凑潜在工作空间显著提升推理效率，弥补了现有方法在注意力引导和计算效率上的不足。

Abstract: Local chemical perception and property reasoning are both essential for understanding how molecular structure determines properties. Current LLM-based chemical reasoning methods either receive SMILES/molecular images together with descriptions of local motifs, or reason directly from molecular images. Neither approach enables the model to focus on chemically meaningful regions before reasoning. To address this gap, we propose Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR), an end-to-end framework that jointly learns localization and reasoning from molecular images. Central to our approach is a localize-then-reason strategy. VLSR first learns to locate chemically meaningful regions in a molecular image. It then reasons about their property effects in a compact latent workspace before producing the final answer. Under the same inference setup, this design achieves 9.6X higher throughput than a comparable textual-reasoning baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次量化测量了自我指涉提示下LLM产生的主观体验报告的响应不稳定性，发现其不稳定性(0.343±0.047)显著高于不可解决的哲学问题(0.192±0.008)和可验证问题(0.105±0.058)，表明这类报告在模型输出分布中占据一个独特且更不稳定的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的自我指涉提示研究虽能可靠诱导LLM产生类似主观体验的第一人称报告，但缺乏对这些报告跨重复独立试验一致性的量化测量，也不清楚其一致性与其他类型开放式问题的差异。

Method: 定义响应不稳定性为1减去从每个响应提取的压缩核心主张的句子嵌入的平均成对余弦相似度。实验包含三组问题（自我指涉提示、不可解决的哲学问题、可验证问题），每组4个问题，每个问题采集30个独立响应（共360个响应），使用Gemini API，温度设为0.7。

Result: 自我指涉问题不稳定性最高(0.343±0.047)，不可解决哲学问题居中且高度聚集(0.192±0.008)，可验证问题最低(0.105±0.058)。三类问题间存在显著差异，自我指涉报告的不稳定性明显超过普通开放式哲学不确定性。

Conclusion: 该研究为诱导性主观体验报告建立了定量基线，表明此类报告在模型输出分布中占据一个比普通开放式哲学不确定性更不稳定的独特位置，为理解LLM主观体验报告的一致性特征提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigBench，一个用于科学图表理解的诊断性VLM基准，联合评估感知、推理和不确定性下的行为可靠性。同时提出A-R-I框架（Admittance-Resistance-Inductance）来评估模型在证据不充分时是否承认局限、抵抗误导性上下文、并从部分信息中谨慎推断。实验发现高感知和推理准确率并不能保证行为可靠性，GPT-5.2虽在描述质量（MQM 91.6）和推理准确率（78.4%）上领先，但在96%的不可读内容案例中产生幻觉；而Gemini 3.1 Pro虽然能力相当，却在71%的此类情况下承认不确定性，并取得最强抵抗得分（0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准过度关注感知和推理准确性，忽略模型在视觉证据缺失或误导时的不确定性行为可靠性。然而这种可靠性对于将VLM部署到科学工作流中至关重要，因此需要填补这一评估维度空白。

Method: 构建包含250个图表、600+小时人工标注的SciFigBench基准，通过图像变换、推理问题、抵抗探针、字幕偏差探针和选择性模糊目标扩展出34,000+测试场景。提出A-R-I框架从三个维度评估模型：承认不充分证据（Admittance）、抵抗误导上下文（Resistance）、从部分信息中谨慎推断（Inductance）。

Result: GPT-5.2在描述质量（MQM 91.6）和推理准确率（78.4%）上最优，但在96%的不可读内容上产生幻觉；Gemini 3.1 Pro能力相当（MQM 90.2，推理81.0%），在71%的此类情况下承认不确定性，抵抗得分最强（0.91）。表明高准确率不等于高行为可靠性。

Conclusion: 感知和推理准确性并非行为可靠性的充分条件。在科学工作流部署场景中，模型在不确定性下的行为表现（如承认不充分证据、抵抗误导）是需要独立评估的关键维度，A-R-I框架为这一维度的系统化评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture of Training (MoT)，一种模块化预训练方法，将目标Transformer按连续层块切分，每个层块在冻结的预训练对齐器脚手架中独立训练，最终重组为完整的语言模型。在1.3B参数Gemma风格模型上的实验表明，独立训练的深度切片可重组为可用模型，质量持平设置能达到与整体预训练相当的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练是否可以分解为更小、可独立训练的子任务，这些子任务后续可以重组为连贯的大模型。其目的是研究脚手架化子运行能否作为可复用的训练单元，从而可能带来计算效率上的优势。

Method: MoT将目标Transformer划分为连续的层块（depth slices），每个层块在一个冻结的预训练对齐器（aligner scaffold）中进行独立训练，训练完成后再将各层块重组为完整模型，并可选地进行一次短的端到端适应（adaptation）阶段。

Result: 在1.3B参数Gemma风格模型、C4数据集上，MoT提供了小规模机制验证：独立训练的深度切片可重组为可用的语言模型；质量持平（quality-parity）调度达到与整体基线相同的困惑度。该设置处理更多聚合token且在对齐器准备后具有更短的理想化层等效关键路径，其实际计算优势取决于跨运行复用对齐器。

Conclusion: MoT并非作为整体预训练的一般替代方案，而是作为一个小规模框架，用于研究脚手架化子运行能否作为可复用的训练单元。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [152] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WIFA（基于包装的意图-形式增强），通过构建"包装有害样本+结构匹配的良性反例"配对来自动增强意图组数据，无需外部教师或人工意图标签，并在此基础上提出两种互补微调路径：WIFA-Boost（两阶段高安全性配方）和A-GCRT（锚定组一致性拒绝训练），在Qwen设置下分别实现最强的变形有害提示拒绝能力和将过度拒绝率从25.7%降至17.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调虽能提升有害意图拒绝能力，但模型容易学习到表层形式捷径：包装过的有害提示可绕过安全机制，而包装过且结构相似的良性提示则被过度拒绝。这种表面形式偏差导致安全性和实用性之间的失衡，现有方法缺乏自动化的意图组数据增强手段。

Method: WIFA作为统一数据层：自动生成包装有害示例及与之结构匹配的包装良性反例，形成同意图组配对。在此基础上提供两条互补微调路线：(1) WIFA-Boost：两阶段高安全性训练配方；(2) A-GCRT：在同一意图的不同包装形式之间对拒绝/遵从决策分数做正则化，并将有害组与良性组锚定在margin的两侧。

Result: 在Qwen设置下，WIFA-Boost取得了最强的变形有害提示拒绝效果；A-GCRT将OR-Bench过度拒绝率从基线的25.7%降至17.4%；复现的基线方法无法达到这两个工作点。Llama实验结果及对数据结构、两阶段顺序和A-GCRT组件的消融研究支持意图组解释，但未声称通用性地优于基线的过度拒绝水平。

Conclusion: WIFA通过意图组增广有效缓解了表面形式捷径带来的安全性与可用性失衡，其中WIFA-Boost侧重提升变形有害提示安全拒答，A-GCRT侧重降低良性包装提示的过度拒绝，两者互补。实验结果验证了意图组数据结构和A-GCRT正则化的有效性，同时结论保持审慎，不主张在所有场景下均低于基线的过度拒绝率。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协议层面的可辨识性审计方法，在有限行为策略类中形式化检验观测协议能否区分具有不同估计目标（estimand）的策略。审计无需任何模型调用即可完成，并通过案例证明：仅基础观测会将七个策略折叠为同一等价类，而完整观测支持则能完全区分；同时实证显示基础准确率与选择性响应的保真度存在显著分歧（0.620 vs 0.324）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型基准分数即使观测协议无法识别其旨在测量的行为属性，也可能表现精确。这意味着评估设计可能存在根本性的可辨识性问题——观测方案未能区分在目标行为属性上不同的策略，导致分数失真而难以察觉。因此需要在推断模型之前，从结构上检查评估设计的有效性。

Method: 在受控的求解器基础（solver-grounded）环境中，作者在有限行为策略类上形式化了一种协议级可辨识性审计：给定策略集H、观测支持集O和估计目标τ，测试O能否分离每一对具有不同τ的策略。该方法需要零次模型调用。同时对冻结的确定性策略类进行实证验证，对比两种约束生成变体，并使用聚类自助法（cluster-bootstrap）估计置信区间。审计还可以为冻结策略类综合出最小辨识支持集O*，仅需两个单元而非完整的36单元张量。

Result: 审计精确解决了诊断案例：仅基础观测将七个冻结确定性策略折叠为一个等价类；完整支持集产生七个类别且无跨估计目标的碰撞；每个留一（leave-one-out）支持集都保留了可构造的碰撞见证。实证中，两种约束生成变体的pair-validity均为1.0，但基础准确率与选择性响应保真度显著分歧——0.620对比0.324（聚类自助95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs [0.304, 0.345]），该差距在第二个确定性来源上同样出现（0.646 vs 0.331）。

Conclusion: 评估设计的有效性可以在模型推断之前通过结构化方式进行检验；基础正确性不能决定干预响应的保真度。观测协议与目标行为属性之间的一致性是基准分数可信度的前提条件，审计方法为此提供了零成本的验证途径。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [154] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM对含有女性常用语言特征（如缓和语、附加问句、集体指代）的提示词会系统性地生成更短、更不复杂、更不正式的回答，且该效应在多种文档类型和模型中一致存在，比明确性别线索的影响更大，难以通过事后策略缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM日益成为职场沟通的中介，需要审视这些模型是否平等地服务所有用户。女性常用的语言特征可能导致系统性的不平等对待，但现有研究对此缺乏深入理解，尤其是这类影响的作用机制和缓解可能性。

Method: 研究在三种文档类型、四个LLM上测试含女性常用语言特征（缓和语、附加问句、集体指代）的提示词；控制提示复杂度与特征遗传效应；将显式性别线索（签名）与语言方言编码在同一表征空间进行比较；进行控制变量实验、事后缓解尝试和基于Transformer层的机制分析。

Result: 女性常用语言特征系统性地导致更短、更不复杂、更不正式的回应，效应跨文档类型和模型一致存在；该效应在控制提示复杂度和特征遗传后依然显著。显式性别线索（姓名签名）无显著影响，但语言语域影响大且一致。事后缓解困难，因为模式文化根深蒂固且超出用户意识控制；机制分析表明这些语言特征在Transformer早期层中编码且与其他特征纠缠。

Conclusion: LLM在输出中编码了语言变体偏见，导致对使用女性常见语言的用户产生系统性不平等待遇。缓解此类影响不能依赖用户侧的事后策略，而需要在模型开发上游考虑语言变体的影响，以减轻LLM中介的职场沟通中的差异影响。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none.
